## 🏷 How to read an entry

```
- [2025] [**CVPR 2025**] UniGoal: Towards Universal Zero-shot Goal-oriented Navigation
  ↑year   ↑venue (bold = top venue)   ↑title (links to paper)
  PDF · project · code · DOI badges, then authors and citation count
```
- **`[2025]`** — publication year
- **`[**CVPR 2025**]`** — published at a **top venue** (CCF-A or a major robotics conference/journal); non-bold = other venue or preprint
- Badges: `arXiv`/`PDF` · `project` · `code` (+ ⭐ stars) · `DOI`

<a id="about"></a>

## 📖 About

**What counts as "zero-shot navigation" here?** Papers where an embodied agent navigates to a goal **without task-specific training** — including zero-shot, training-free, open-vocabulary, and foundation-model / VLA-driven approaches that generalize to unseen goals or environments.

**Data sources** (all free, no API key needed)

| Source | What it adds |
|--------|--------------|
| [OpenAlex](https://openalex.org) | primary harvest (title/abstract search) |
| [Crossref](https://crossref.org) | publication metadata, DOI |
| [OpenReview](https://openreview.net) | conference submissions & acceptance |
| [Hugging Face Papers](https://huggingface.co/papers) | trending signal |
| [Semantic Scholar](https://semanticscholar.org) | citation counts, TLDRs |
| [Unpaywall](https://unpaywall.org) | open-access PDFs |
| [GitHub API](https://github.com) | code repositories |

> ℹ️ **arXiv's own API is not used** — it returns HTTP 406 for requests from cloud/datacenter IPs (GitHub Actions included), regardless of headers or HTTP/HTTPS. Verified with `scripts/probe_sources.py`.

**How it works**

```
Every week (GitHub Actions — free, runs in the cloud, no server needed):
  1. Harvest  → OpenAlex + Crossref (35 queries) + OpenReview + Hugging Face
  2. Filter   → rule-based relevance check (self-tested)
  3. Dedup    → DOI / arXiv ID / fuzzy title matching
  4. Enrich   → citations (S2) · OA PDFs (Unpaywall) · code repos (GitHub) · venue tiers
  5. Export   → this README + docs/data/papers.json
  6. Deploy   → GitHub Pages
```

See **[OPERATIONS.md](OPERATIONS.md)** for where it runs, how often, how the incremental update avoids gaps, and how the 60-day inactivity trap is handled.

**Contributing** — Found a missing paper, a wrong venue, or a mis-classification?

Open an [issue](../../issues) with the paper link and we'll fold it in. The whole pipeline lives in [`scripts/`](scripts) and is plain Python (standard library only — no dependencies to install).

**License** — MIT (list); each paper belongs to its authors.
